\n\n

a + b = 2c
\nFormula used:

\n\n

x - y = -(y - x)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

If a + b = 2c, then a - c = c - b.

\n\n

Then, \\(\\frac{a}{{a - c}} + \\frac{c}{{b - c}} = ​​\\frac{a}{{c - b}} + \\frac{c}{{b - c}} = -\\frac{a}{{b- c}} + \\frac{c}{{b - c}}\\)

\n\n

∴ \\(\\frac{a}{{a - c}} + \\frac{c}{{b - c}} = \\frac{-a+c}{{b - c}} = \\frac{b - c}{{b - c}} = 1\\)

\n\n

∴ If a + b = 2c, then the value of \\(\\frac{a}{{a - c}} + \\frac{c}{{b - c}}\\) is 1.

\n\n

Shortcut TrickWe can use Value putting method here.

\n\n

\n\n

So,

\n\n

⇒ a + b = 0

\n\n

∴ a = -b

\n\n

then, the question being asked shrinks down to → a/a = 1.

\n\n

\n\n

 a + b = 2c எனில், பிறகு a - c = c - b.

Given:

\n\n

\\(x\\)\\(\\left(5-\\frac{2}{x}\\right)\\)= \\(\\frac{5}{x} \\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

(a + b)2  = a2 + 2ab + b2

\n\n

(a + b)3  = a3 + 3ab(a + b) + b3

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\\(x\\)\\(\\left(5-\\frac{2}{x}\\right)\\)= \\(\\frac{5}{x} \\)

\n\n

⇒ 5\\(x\\) - 2 = \\(\\frac{5}{x} \\)

\n\n

⇒ 5\\(x\\) - \\(\\frac{5}{x} \\) = 2

\n\n

⇒ 5(\\(x\\) - 1/\\(x\\)) = 2

\n\n

⇒ (\\(x\\) - 1/\\(x\\)) = 2/5

\n\n

Squaring both sides

\n\n

⇒ \\(x\\)2 + 1/\\(x\\)2 - 2 = 4/25

\n\n

⇒ \\(x\\)2 + 1/\\(x\\)2 = 4/25 + 2

\n\n

⇒ \\(x\\)2 + 1/\\(x\\)2 = 54/25

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Standard Identity: If x3 + y3 + z3 = 3xyz, then x + y + z = 0.

\n

Given equation: 8a3 + b3 + 27c3 = 18abc

\n

Rewrite as: (2a)3 + (b)3 + (3c)3 = 3 × (2a) × (b) × (3c)

\n

Here x = 2a, y = b, and z = 3c.

\n

⇒ 2a + b + 3c = 0 ⇒ b + 3c = −2a

\n

∴ The correct answer is b + 3c = −2a.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

8a3 + b3 + 27c3 = 18abc

\n

Formula Used:

\n

x3 + y3 + z3 − 3xyz = (x + y + z)(x2 + y2 + z2 − xy − yz − zx)

\n

If x3 + y3 + z3 = 3xyz, then either x + y + z = 0 or x = y = z.

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ The given expression can be arranged as follows by bringing all terms to one side:

\n

⇒ 8a3 + b3 + 27c3 − 18abc = 0

\n

⇒ Now, represent each term as a perfect cube:

\n

⇒ (2a)3 + (b)3 + (3c)3 − 3 × (2a) × (b) × (3c) = 0

\n

⇒ Comparing this directly with the algebraic identity, we can substitute x = 2a, y = b, and z = 3c.

\n

⇒ According to the identity rule, for the expression to be zero, the sum of the base variables must be zero.

\n

⇒ x + y + z = 0

\n

⇒ 2a + b + 3c = 0

\n

⇒ Moving 2a to the right side of the equation:

\n

⇒ b + 3c = −2a

\n

This strictly matches the relation given in the options.

\n

∴ The correct answer is b + 3c = −2a.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Core Algebraic Identity

\n

a3 + b3 + c3 − 3abc = (a + b + c)(a2 + b2 + c2 − ab − bc − ca).

\n

Conditional Cubes Identity

\n

If a + b + c = 0, then substituting this into the main identity simplifies to a3 + b3 + c3 = 3abc.

\n

Sum of Squares Form

\n

a2 + b2 + c2 − ab − bc − ca = ½[(a − b)2 + (b − c)2 + (c − a)2].

\n

Equality Condition

\n

If a3 + b3 + c3 = 3abc and a + b + c ≠ 0, it strictly means a = b = c.

\n\n

(xn + 1/xn) identity method

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 18 × 322 = x6 + 1/x6 + 18

\n\n

⇒ x6 + 1/x6 = 5796 − 18 = 5778

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ a + b + c = 0

\n\n

then,

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{a^2b^2}{a^2+b^2-ab}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ఇచ్చినవి​:

\n\n

(1+x)/(1-x²) ÷ (1+x)/(1-x⁴) - (1-x⁴)/(1-x) * (1+x)/(1-x²).

\n\n

పరిష్కారం:

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ యొక్క విభజన భాగాన్ని సరళీకృతం చేయడం ద్వారా ప్రారంభించండి: (1+x)/(1-x²) ÷ (1+x)/(1-x⁴).

\n\n

(1+x)/(1-x²) ×  (1-x⁴)/(1+x)

\n\n

లవము మరియు హారం నుండి (1+x) రద్దు చేయడం వల్ల మనకు (1-x²)/(1-x⁴) వస్తుంది.

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ యొక్క గుణకార భాగాన్ని సులభతరం చేయండి: (1-x⁴)/(1-x) * (1+x)/(1-x²).

\n\n

 [(1-x²)(1+x²)]/(1-x) * (1+x)/(1-x²)

\n\n

⇒ లవము  మరియు హారం నుండి (1-x²) రద్దు చేయడం మనకు (1+x²)/(1-x) ఇస్తుంది.

\n\n

దశ 1 నుండి ఫలితం నుండి దశ 2 నుండి ఫలితాన్ని తీసివేయండి: (1-x²)/(1-x⁴) - (1+x²)/(1-x).

\n\n

దీనికి సాధారణ హారం అవసరం, ఈ సందర్భంలో ఇది (1-x)(1-x³).

\n\n

[(1-x²)(1+x) - (1-x³)(1+x²)] / [(1-x)(1-x³)].

\n\n

లవము 2x(1+x²)కి సులభతరం చేస్తుంది మరియు హారం (1-x)(1-x³)కి సులభతరం చేస్తుంది, ఇది మనకు 2x(1+x²) / [(1-x)(1-x³)] ఇస్తుంది.

\n\n

(x + y)2 = (x - y)2 + 4xy

\n\n

x2 - y2 = (x + y)(x - y)

\n\n

गणना-

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 27 + 9xy = 999

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 63

Given:

\n\n

x + y + z = 25

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 = 85

\n\n

xyz = 20

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

On put the value given in the question, and we get

\n\n

⇒ x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x + y + z)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ 85 - 3(20) = (25)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ (x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)(x + y + z) = 85 - 60 = 25

\n\n

⇒ x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx = 25 / 25 = 1

\n\n

दिया है:

\n\n

x + y + z = 25

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 = 85

\n\n

xyz = 20

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x+y+z)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रश्न में दिया गया मान रखने पर, हमें प्राप्त होता है:

\n\n

⇒ x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x + y + z)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ 85 - 3(20) = (25)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ (x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)(x + y + z) = 85 - 60 = 25

\n\n

⇒ x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx = 25 / 25 = 1

\n\n

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

x + y + z = 25

\n\n

xఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

x + y + z = 25

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 = 85

\n\n

xyz = 20

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x+y+z)(xदिलेल्याप्रमाणे:

\n\n

x + y + z = 25

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 = 85

\n\n

xyz = 20

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x+y+z)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रश्नात दिलेले मूल्य टाका आणि आपल्याला मिळेल

\n\n

⇒ x3 + y3 + z3 - 3xyz = (x + y + z)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ 85 - 3(20) = (25)(x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)

\n\n

⇒ (x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx)(x + y + z) = 85 - 60 = 25

\n\n

⇒ x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx = 25 / 25 = 1

\n\n

∴ x2 + y2 + z2 - xy - yz - zx चे मूल्य 1 आहे.

Shortcut Trick\n

If x + 1/x = p, then x − 1/x = √(p2 − 4).

\n

Given p = 2√10, so x − 1/x = √(40 − 4) = √36 = 6.

\n

If x − 1/x = k, then x3 − 1/x3 = k3 + 3k.

\n

Substituting k = 6:

\n

x3 − 1/x3 = 63 + 3 × 6 = 216 + 18 = 234.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 234.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

x + 1/x = 2√10, where x > 1

\n

Formula Used:

\n

(x − 1/x)2 = (x + 1/x)2 − 4

\n

x3 − 1/x3 = (x − 1/x)3 + 3(x − 1/x)

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ (x − 1/x)2 = (2√10)2 − 4

\n

⇒ (x − 1/x)2 = 4 × 10 − 4

\n

⇒ (x − 1/x)2 = 40 − 4 = 36

\n

⇒ Since x > 1, x − 1/x will be positive. So, x − 1/x = √36 = 6

\n

⇒ Now, substitute this into the cube formula:

\n

⇒ x3 − 1/x3 = (6)3 + 3 × 6

\n

⇒ x3 − 1/x3 = 216 + 18

\n

⇒ x3 − 1/x3 = 234

\n

∴ The correct answer is 234.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Square Rule

\n

If x + 1/x = a, then x2 + 1/x2 = a2 − 2.

\n

Cube Rule (Plus)

\n

If x + 1/x = a, then x3 + 1/x3 = a3 − 3a.

\n

Sign Conversion Rule

\n

If x + 1/x = a, then x − 1/x = √(a2 − 4). Similarly, if x − 1/x = a, then x + 1/x = √(a2 + 4).

दिया गया है:

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

(a + 1/a)2 - (a - 1/a)2 = 4

\n\n

(a - 1/a)3 = a3 - (1/a)3 - 3(a - 1/a)

\n\n

\n\n

(x + 1/x)2 - (x - 1/x)2 = 4

\n\n

⇒ (x - 1/x)2 = (x + 1/x)2 - 4

\n\n

मान रखने पर x + 1/x = 2√10,

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ (x - 1/x) = √36 = 6

\n\n

⇒ (x - 1/x)3 = x3 - 1/x3 - 3(x - 1/x)

\n\n

मान रखने पर (x - 1/x) = 6

\n\n

⇒ x3 - 1/x3 = (6)3 + 3 x 6 = 216 + 18 = 234

\n\n

∴ x3 - 1/x3 = 234

சூத்திரத்தைப் பயன்படுத்தி: (a + b) 2 = a 2 + b 2 + 2ab

\n\n

⇒ x 2 + 4/x 2 + 2 × x × 2/x = 49

\n\n

⇒ x 2 + 4/x 2 + 4 = 49

\n\n

⇒ x 2 + 4/x 2 = 49 - 4

\n\n

⇒ x 2 + 4/x 2   = 45

\n\n

சமன்பாட்டின் இரு பக்கங்களிலும் 2 ஆல் பெருக்கினால், நமக்குக் கிடைக்கும்

\n\n

⇒ 2 × (x 2 + 4/x 2 ) = 45 × 2

\n\n

⇒ 2x 2 + 8/x 2 = 90

\n\n

∴ (2x 2 + 8/x 2 ) இன் மதிப்பு 90 ஆகும்.

Given:

\n\n

\\(x+\\frac{1}{3x}=5\\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

(a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3 × a × b × (a + b)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\\(x+\\frac{1}{3x}=5\\)

\n\n

Multiplying both sides by 3, we get

\n\n

⇒ 3x + 1/x = 15

\n\n

Now,

\n\n

Taking the cube on both sides,

\n\n

⇒ (3x)3 + 1/x3 = (15)3

\n\n

⇒ 27x3 + 1/x3दिया गया है:

\n\n

\\(x+\\frac{1}{3x}=5\\)

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

(a + b)3 = a3 + b3 + 3 × a × b × (a + b)

Given:

\n\n

x = 3 + 2√2

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

(a2 – b2) = (a – b)(a + b)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Given that 

\n\n

x = 3 + 2√2               ----(1)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{1}{x} = \\frac{1}{3+2√ 2} \\times \\frac{3 - 2√2}{3 -2√ 2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{1}{x} = \\frac{3 - 2√2}{9 -8} = 3-2√2\\)     ---(2)

\n\n

⇒ x + 1/x = (3 + 2√2) + (3 - 2√2) 

\n\n

⇒ x + 1/x = 6              ----(3)

\n\n

⇒ (x + 1/x)2 = x2 + 1/x2 + 2

\n\n

⇒ (6)2 = x2 + 1/x2 + 2

\n\n

⇒ x2 + 1/x2 = 36 - 2 = 34

\n\n

Alternate Method x = 3 + 2√2

\n\n

Squaring on both sides

\n\n

x2= (3 + 2√2)2

\n\n

x2 = 32 + 2 × 3 × 2√2 + (2√2)2

\n\n

x2= 9 + 12√2 + 8

\n\n

x2= 17 + 12√2      ----1

\n\n

To find   \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\)

\n\n

\\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\; = \\;17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 + {\\rm{\\;}}\\frac{1}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{\\rm{\\;\\;\\;\\;\\;}}\\)

\n\n

By rationalisation method 

\n\n

⇒ \\(17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 {\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}\\frac{1}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\; × \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\)

\n\n

Use (a2 – b2) = (a – b)(a + b)

\n\n

⇒ \\(17 + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} \\; + \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{{{{17}^2}\\; - \\;{{(12\\surd 2)}^2}}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} + \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} \\;}}{{289\\; - \\;288}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 17 + 12√2 + 17 – 12√2

\n\n

⇒ 34

\n\n

⇒  \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}} = 34\\;\\)

\n\n

∴ The value of \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\) is 34

x = 3 + 2√2

वापरलेले सूत्र:

(a 2 – b 2 ) = (a – b)(a + b)

गणना:

ते दिले

x = 3 + 2√2 ----(1)

⇒ \\(\\frac{1}{x} = \\frac{1}{3+2√ 2} \\times \\frac{3 - 2√2}{3 -2√ 2}\\)

⇒ \\(\\frac{1}{x} = \\frac{3 - 2√2}{9 -8} = 3-2√2\\) ---(2)

⇒ x + 1/x = (3 + 2√2) + (3 - 2√2)

⇒ x + 1/x = 6 ----(3)

⇒ (x + 1/x) 2 = x 2 + 1/x 2 + 2

⇒ (6) 2 = x 2 + 1/x 2 + 2

⇒ x 2 + 1/x 2 = 36 - 2 = 34

पर्यायी पद्धत x = 3 + 2√2

दोन्ही बाजूंना चौरस

x 2 = (3 + 2√2) 2

x 2 = 3 2 + 2 × 3 × 2√2 + (2√2) 2

x 2 = 9 + 12√2 + 8

x 2 = 17 + 12√2 ----1

शोधण्यासाठी \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\)

\\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\; = \\;17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 + {\\rm{\\;}}\\frac{1}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{\\rm{\\;\\;\\;\\;\\;}}\\)

तर्कशुद्धीकरण पद्धतीद्वारे

⇒\\(17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 {\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}\\frac{1}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\; × \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\)

वापरा (a 2 – b 2 ) = (a – b)(a + b)

⇒ \\(17 + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} \\; + \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{{{{17}^2}\\; - \\;{{(12\\surd 2)}^2}}}\\)

⇒ \\(17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} + \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ {2\\;} \\;}}{{289\\; - \\;288}}\\)

⇒ 17 + 12√2 + 17 – 12√2

⇒ ३४

⇒ \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}} = 34\\;\\)

∴ \\({x^2} + \\frac{1}{{{x^2}}}\\) चे मूल्य 34 आहे

ಬಳಸಿದ ಸೂತ್ರ:

(a 2 – b 2 ) = (a – b)(a + b)

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

ಎಂದು ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ

x = 3 + 2√2 ----(1)

⇒ \\(\\frac{1}{x} = \\frac{1}{3+2√ 2} \\times \\frac{3 - 2√2}{3 -2√ 2}\\)

⇒ x + 1/x = (3 + 2√2) + (3 - 2√2)

⇒ x + 1/x = 6 ----(3)

⇒ (x + 1/x) 2 = x 2 + 1/x 2 + 2

⇒ (6) 2 = x 2 + 1/x 2 + 2

⇒ x 2 + 1/x 2 = 36 - 2 = 34

ಪರ್ಯಾಯ ವಿಧಾನ x = 3 + 2√2

ಎರಡೂ ಬದಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಚೌಕ

x 2 = (3 + 2√2) 2

x 2 = 3 2 + 2 × 3 × 2√2 + (2√2) 2

x 2 = 9 + 12√2 + 8

ತರ್ಕಬದ್ಧಗೊಳಿಸುವ ವಿಧಾನದಿಂದ

⇒\\(17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 {\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}\\frac{1}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} + {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\; × \\;\\frac{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}{{17{\\rm{\\;}} - {\\rm{\\;}}12√ 2 }}\\)

⇒ 17 + 12√2 + 17 – 12√2

⇒ 34
